# Trading Cockpit Traceability Matrix

**Work package:** `TC-IMP-BASE-03`
**Baseline ID:** `HQA-TC-P0-20260807T075707Z-3b039544`
**Captured (UTC):** `2026-08-07T07:57:07Z`
**Source specification:** `docs/dev/trading-cockpit/Trading_Cockpit_Game_Specification_v1.2.md` (SHA-256 `b460f314f0fdf6827af381278f917648ad36f3f82c9f27942e04b0ebbc97889c`)
**Machine-readable twin:** `trading-cockpit-traceability-matrix.csv` (603 rows)

## Decomposition method

The specification was decomposed into 603 atomic requirements using four extraction passes over the
source document. Source IDs were preserved wherever the specification already assigned one.

| Requirement class | Count | Identifier form | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Checklist steps | 125 | `PRE_nnn`, `ENTRY_nnn`, `MGT_nnn`, `EXIT_nnn`, `POST_nnn` | Specification section 2 |
| Emergency steps | 24 | `FLASH_nnn`, `API_nnn`, `DD_nnn` | Specification section 3 |
| Normative prose statements | 137 | `TCS-Snn-REQ-nnn` (derived) | Sections 0-17 |
| Normative table rows | 277 | `TCS-Snn-m-TBL-nnn` (derived) | Sections 5-17 |
| Final acceptance criteria | 40 | `TCS-AC-001` .. `TCS-AC-040` | Section 17.3 |
| **Total** | **603** | | |

Derived identifiers are stable: they are a function of section number, subsection number and ordinal
position within the source document. No source requirement was rewritten or weakened; the
`Normative Requirement` column carries the source wording (table rows are joined with ` / `).

## Coverage confirmation

- All 125 explicit checklist steps in sections 2.1 through 2.5 appear exactly once.
- All 24 emergency steps in sections 3.1 through 3.3 appear exactly once.
- All 40 final acceptance criteria (`TCS-AC-001` .. `TCS-AC-040`) appear exactly once.

## Current implementation status counts

| Status | Count |
|---|---|
| `FULL` | 0 |
| `PARTIAL` | 353 |
| `ABSENT` | 250 |
| `CONFLICTING` | 0 |
| `UNKNOWN` | 0 |
| `NOT_APPLICABLE` | 0 |
| **Total** | **603** |

No requirement is `FULL`: the Trading Cockpit does not exist in the repository. `PARTIAL` rows are those
where a genuine, located repository capability covers part of the requirement; the `Current Evidence`
column gives the exact path and symbol and the `Conflict/Unknown` column states precisely what is missing.

## Primary owner distribution

| Owner domain | Requirements |
|---|---|
| Simulator | 120 |
| UI-API | 92 |
| Trading | 85 |
| Portfolio | 67 |
| Analytics | 56 |
| Brokers | 55 |
| Risk | 51 |
| Phase-15 QA | 23 |
| Research | 17 |
| Indicators | 13 |
| Strategy | 12 |
| Data | 10 |
| Agentic | 2 |

`Utils` and `Optimization` hold no directly-owned normative requirement. Both are support domains whose
obligations arrive through the work-package plan (`TC-IMP-UTIL-01..12`, `TC-IMP-OPT-01..10`) rather than
through a directly attributable normative sentence in the specification. This is recorded rather than
resolved.
